Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

曜越(3540)：2027 年 Gaming PC 與 AI 工 
建議：評等買進

一、曜越為電競電腦零組件大廠，積極切入AI 高效能工
作站系統 
曜越科技股份有限公司成立於 1999 年 11 月，為電腦機
殼、散熱器(模組)及電源供應器製造商，以自有品牌
Thermaltake 銷售桌上型電腦散熱模組、機殼、電源供
應器，專攻 clone 遊戲玩家市場，近年來公司積極整合
機殼、散熱、電源與系統配置能力，從單一產品銷售延
伸至完整 Gaming PC 系統解決方案，滿足高階 
Gaming PC 的效能與穩定性需求。4Q26 公司將切入
AI 工作站(AI Workstation)新業務 ，拓展地端 AI 運算
市場。2026 年 1~9 月主要產品營收占比，Gaming PC 
35%、電源供應器 33%、機殼 14%、散熱 13%、其他
5%。

二、2Q26 本業疲弱，EPS 0.01 元 
曜越 2Q26 營收 10.16 億元，年減 20.5%，毛利率則從
前期 28.1%升至 30.7%，主要是因記憶體漲價，使毛利
率較佳的客製化裝機業務佔比提升， 2Q26 稅後淨利
96.8 萬元，EPS 0.01 元。 
三、曜越從 PC 零組件品牌轉型為系統整合品牌，並積
極切入 AI 工作站市場  
展望下半年 ，3Q26 營收年減 2.9%， 估計3Q26 稅後 EPS 
0.09 元。4Q 通常為傳統旺季，加上 AI 工作站從雲端到
地端的基本條件已於 2026 年第三季成熟，產品規劃完
成，第四季開始進入市場，出貨量將逐步上升。 
2026 年前九月 Gaming PC 營收佔比已達 35%， 超越電
源供應器的 33%，成為最大產品線，象徵系統整合業務
已成為營收核心。曜越的電競 PC 分為三個價格層次：
入門級 1,599-1,799 美元、進階級 2,599 美元、高階
4,399-4,499 美元。其中核心零組件如機殼、散熱、電源
皆是自製，而主機板、CPU、GPU、SSD 等為外購，透
過美國、德國、澳洲、中國等海外子公司進行在地組裝
與銷售。展望下一階段，曜越將複製電競 PC 的成功模
式，推出五款 AI 工作站與 AI PC 產品線，從入門級 AI
電競 PC（View 380 XL，1,801 TOPS）到企業旗艦雙
系統 AI 工作站（A1500 Dual，16,000 TOPS） ，搭載
NVIDIA RTX 5090、RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 及 AMD 
Radeon AI PRO R9700 等高階 GPU。AI 工作站從雲端
到地端的基本條件已於 2026 年第三季成熟，產品規劃
完成，第四季開始進入市場，2027 年營收貢獻將逐季顯
現 ， 目標2027 年占整體業績一成 ， 主要鎖定AI 開發者、
專業創作者、學術科研及中小企業等應用。這代表曜越
正式從傳統電競跨足企業級 AI 運算、專業開發與數位
創作的地端商機，由於商用工作站毛利通常優於一般消
費級產品，這將是明年拉升獲利結構與擴展新市場的最
大看點。 
四、評等買進理由 
投顧認為曜越已從機箱、電源供應器及散熱等 PC 零組
件，轉型至拓展更高毛利率的電競PC 系統整合與 AI 工
作站業務，使 2026 年整體毛利率有望升到 30%以上，
預期 2027 年營收將成長 15%，估計 2026 年 EPS 0.46
元，2027 年 EPS 1.95 元，給予買進的建議，目標價31
元。(16x2027EPS)。

營收走勢圖

研究員/游周翰 Jeffery.Yu@uni-psg.com

研究部重申華星光買進建議，主要考量：(1)管理層維持下半年營運優
於上半年展望，受惠 800G DCI 及 CW Laser 需求持續成長，4Q26 單月
營收有望挑戰 6 億元；(2)隨晶粒稼動率及良率持續改善，研究部上修
2027 年晶粒出貨量預估，加上 EML 等產品逐步放量，有望帶動產品組
合優化及獲利能力提升。綜上所述，研究部預估華星光 2026/2027 年
營收，由原先 60.6 億元/119.8 億元調整為 58.4 億元/120.8 億元
(+33%/107%YoY)，EPS 由原先 7.69 元/15.88 元調整為 7.5 元/16.35
元。考量2027 年產能擴充及相關產品出貨成長，營收與獲利有望呈現
倍數成長，維持目標價 800 元的買進建議。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26營收優於研究部預期，惟受匯率影響EPS僅符合預期： 
華星光於 10/14 公 佈 自 結 ，9 月營收 5.75 億元（ +5.3%MoM 、
+97.8%YoY） ，稅後淨利1.03億元，稅後淨利率達17.9%，EPS達
0.72元。相較於8月回升，除了匯率回穩外，亦受到營收規模放
大所影響 。 累計3Q26營收為16.29億元 （+26.4%QoQ，+51.4%YoY），
稅後淨利達3.26億元，EPS為2.28元，創歷史新高。若依研究部
過去費用率及業外假設回推，3Q26毛利率約28-29%（約+6ppts 
QoQ） ，主要受惠先前財稅認列時間差回補、產能利用率提升及
產品組合優化 。 整體而言 ，3Q26營收優於研究部預期16.19億元 ，
EPS僅符合預期2.26元 ， 推測主因受到匯率所影響毛利率及業外
損益。

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 下半年營運優於上半年，惟毛利率受新產品良率等因素影響 ： 
展望4Q26， 管理層維持2H26營運優於1H26， 除了既有DCI產品持
續成長外，CW Laser需求持續提升、800G DCI進入批量生產，
以及EML與高增益APD開始貢獻營收，有望帶動整體營運向上，
單月營收有望挑戰6億元 。 近期經研究部通路訪查 ，800GZR出貨
量略低於原先預估，因此研究部下修 4Q26出貨量從75K下修至
55K，4Q26營收從原先預估19.6億元下修至17.31億元。此外考
量800GZR毛利率因良率及協助客戶購買稀釋毛利率不如原先研
究部預期的情況下，將EPS從2.51元下修至2.29元。

3. 隨產能擴充，營收獲利將持續成長： 
展望未來，在2027年產能倍增情況下，營收規模有望呈現倍數
成長。晶粒方面，除了既有的CW Laser產能擴充外，亦增加EML
晶粒產能，使公司在InP產品線有多種產品分散風險。此外，近
期經供應鏈訪查，晶粒稼動率及良率提升下，有望使整體晶粒
出貨量優於原先預期。因此研究部預估2027年整體晶粒出貨量
從4700萬顆上修至5000萬顆。綜上所述，研究部預估營收2027
年營收從119.8億元上修至120.8億元 ， EPS從15.88元微幅上修
至16.35元。

資料來源： 華星光自結、統一研究部預估

本刊載之報告為元富投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬元富投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (1 15)金管投顧新字第 016 號 
公司拜訪快報

台半(5425) 
建議：維持買進 
結論：2027 年產品漲價效益將更全面

一、台半為功率元件製造與封測廠商，車用營收占比最
高 
台半成立於 1979 年並為功率元件製造與封測廠商，晶
圓製造有宜蘭六吋廠和天津四吋廠，後段封測則有天津
和山東兩座廠房，半導體以及條碼印表機(子公司鼎漢
3611 TT, 持股 35.74%)分別占 1H26 營收比重為 38%和
62%，半導體業務主要產品包含 MOSFET、保護元件
TVS 和 ESD、二極體，MOSFET 產品線主要透過和晶
圓代工廠如聯電合作，非 MOSFET 產品線則以自製為
主。若以產品應用而言，車用、工業、3C 和其他分別占
1H26 營收比重為 59%、16%、23%和 2%。因MOSFET
業務拓展有成，營收占比由 2024 年的中個位數提升至
目前 20%，且車用客戶結構由中系擴展至歐美客戶，產
品組合優化為毛利率改善另一主因。 
二、報價逐季調整，2H26 營收獲利可望季季高 
除 7 月調整部分產品價格外，鼎翰併購 Bluebird 綜效顯
現並開始出貨大型企業專案，預估 3Q25 營收 52.35 億
元(+3%QoQ)，受惠於無一次性晶圓報廢影響以及產品
漲價效益，毛利率將由上季之 29.6%提升至 30.8%，營
業利益 5.57 億元(+18%QoQ)，稅後淨利 2.53 億元
(+25%QoQ)，EPS 1.03 元。展望 4Q26，受惠於更多品
項產品將於 11 月漲價，預期 4Q26 營收 53.53 億元
(+2%QoQ)，預估毛利率 31.7%優於前季之 30.8%，營
業利益 6.26 億元(+13%QoQ)，稅後淨利 3 億元
(+19%QoQ)，EPS 1.22 元  
三、去中化轉單效益，以及AI 強勁需求改善產業供需為
營運成長兩大動能 
去年 10 月安世半導體之亂以及今年 4 月揚杰科技被歐
盟列入制裁名單，皆引發全球功率半導體去中化轉單效
應，此兩家業者與台半產品重疊性高，其中安世半導體
轉單效益推估增加單季營收達 3-4 百萬美元，因車用客
戶驗證時程冗長，後續訂單延續性高，至於揚杰科技轉
單效益，經營階層認為尚不顯著。此外，Onsemi 於法
說會上將 AI 資料中心功率半導體市場規模由原先預估
的 120 億美元大幅調升至 500 億美元，雖 AI 營收僅占
公司營收個位數 ， 但AI 外溢需求致使成熟製程產能排擠，
改善功率元件產業供需平衡與價格，且國際 IDM 大廠
將資源轉移至 AI 用高瓦數電源模組與高階 SiC/GaN，
此亦致使車用與工控 MOSFET、二極體供給較過去吃
緊。 
四、產品漲價效益尚未完全反映 
全球功率半導體去中化轉單效應 ， 且今年以來Infineon、
TI、NX、STM 以及士蘭微等多家大廠陸續調漲報價，皆
反映出功率元件市場供需改善，且銅、金價格居高不下
致使成本增加，加計委外晶圓代工和封測成本皆增加，
故致使台半今年 4、7 和 11 月三次調漲報價，但今年產
品漲價效益僅限於產能占比近半之非合約客戶。投顧認
為台半今年毛利率改善幅度來自於稼動率提升勝於產品
漲價因素 ， 然功率元件產業受惠去中化轉單和AI 強勁需
求趨勢不變，加計反應委外晶圓代工和封測上揚成本，
預期 2027 年台半產品漲價效應將擴及部分合約客戶。  
五、維持買進理由 
1)預估 2026 年和 2027 年 EPS 分別為 3.78 元和 5.89
元。 
2)考量目前產品上漲僅限於非合約 ，2027 年產品漲價效
益更加全面，給予買進評等，TP 130 元 (2027 PER x 
22)。

億豐(8464) 
買進, TP 450 (潛在報酬 25.52%)  
買進 
Price(10/20):358.5 
Target Price：450

隨客製化需求持續成長、柬埔寨廠人力恢復至5,000人並開始協助
客戶回補庫存，帶動3Q26營收創下歷史新高，且受惠於營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升及關稅返還挹注，淨利率有望創高。展望後
市，公司持續擴增墨西哥產能，員工人數已達1,800人，後續預計
每年增加約 500 人，以改善交期並滿足客製化窗簾訂單成長需求；
越南新廠預計 2027年初投產，第一期規劃員工約 1,000人，可望
進一步擴大客製化產品產能與市佔率。市場需求方面，儘管美國市
場面臨高通膨及消費降級壓力，但公司憑藉穩健的財務結構，客製
化產品與同業價差已擴大至 30%～40%，具價格競爭優勢，有助
於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預期維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品推出，支撐營收維持 low-single digit成長；歐
洲市場方面，隨荷蘭、比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，可望延續
high-single digit 成長。獲利方面，受惠於關稅稅率降至 20%、
漲價及營收規模效益逐步顯現，預期毛利率將延續成長趨勢。綜合
以上，研究部預估 2026、2027 年 EPS 為 23.92、27.91 元，以
16倍本益比評價，給予目標價 450元，投資建議為買進。  
 近期投資評等  2Q26 毛利率創高，惟油價及運價上漲壓抑獲利表現 
2Q26 營收 79.27 億元，YoY+2.86%，QoQ+8.52%，稅後損
益 13.91 億元，YoY+20.67%，QoQ-15.57%，EPS 4.75 元，
低於研究部預期。第二季為傳統消費旺季，營運表現較第一季
明顯回升。區域營收方面，北美市場受到消費降級影響，加上
柬埔寨廠人力短缺，營收YoY-2.1%；非美市場則受惠於荷蘭、
比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，營收 YoY+19.8%，成為主
要成長動能。獲利方面，2Q26 毛利率 59.95%，YoY+0.7ppt，
QoQ+0.9ppt，受惠於客制化及歐洲市場營收比重持續提升，
帶動毛利率創歷史新高，惟油價及燃油附加費急漲，使費用率
升至 32.7%，抵銷部分毛利率改善效益，壓抑整體獲利表現。

 柬埔寨廠產能恢復，固定尺寸產品開始回補庫存 
展望 3Q26，9M26 營收 26.62 億元 ，YoY+13.5%，MoM-5.5%，
累計前 9 月營收 237.14 億元，YoY+6.3%，優於研究部預期。
第三季營收創歷史新高，台幣營收 YoY+16%、美金營收
YoY+9%，主要受惠於客制化需求持續成長，且柬埔寨廠已補
足至 5,000 人，開始協助客戶補庫存。獲利方面，隨營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升，加上關稅返還挹注，預估3Q26 毛利

現金股利(去年) 14.5 率將優於去年同期；儘管油價及燃油附加費仍高於去年同期，
但較第二季小幅回落，加上營收規模擴大有助於費用率下降，

YoY+14.44%，QoQ+40.42%，EPS 6.67 元。

 越南新廠預計 1Q27投產，挹注未來 3~4年營運成長 
公司客製化窗簾產能主要布局於中國及墨西哥，分別占整體產
能約 60%及 40%。中國廠員工人數約 4,000~4,500 人；墨西
哥則鎖定北美市場，截至 2026 年 9 月，舊廠與新廠合計員工
已達 1,800 人，預計逐年增加約 500 名員工，以改善交期問
題並因應市佔擴張需求，透過規模經濟及生產效率提升，進一
步優化整體獲利結構。柬埔寨廠則以固定尺寸窗簾為主，1H26
受缺工影響，出貨進度放緩， 6M26 起員工人數已由先前的
4,000 人回升至 5,000人，並開始協助客戶回補庫存至健康水
位。越南新廠預計於 2027 年初投產，聚焦客制化產品，第一
期規劃員工人數約 1,000 人，為中長期成長動能。

 同業價差擴大提升競爭力，歐洲市場成長動能延續 
儘管美國消費市場面臨高通膨、消費降級及關稅等壓力，但公
司憑藉穩健的財務結構，與同業的價差已擴大至30%～40%，
加上產品持續迭代更新，有助於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預
估 2026~2027 年客製化業務可維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品免打孔窗廉推出，提高平均售價，以維持
low-single digit 成長。歐洲市場方面，採線上直營電商模式，
專業人員到府量測與安裝服務，由於服務利潤率較高，已成為
主要獲利來源。目前已拓展至荷蘭、比利時、丹麥等市場，隨
覆蓋率快速提升，預期非美地區將延續 high-single digit 成
長。電動窗簾方面，價格為手動款的 2～2.5 倍，儘管目前佔
比仍為 low-single digit，但 CAGR 達 15%～20%以上，預期
未來營收占比有望提升至 10%，進一步帶動整體 ASP 及產品
組合優化。

 漲價效益與產品組合優化，支撐毛利率續創高 
原料成本方面，受美伊戰爭反覆升溫影響，PVC 價格維持 850
美元/噸的相對高檔，然公司近年持續調整配方並提高環保材
料使用比重，降低對 PVC 價格波動的依賴，以減輕短期原料

成本變動對獲利的衝擊。此外，公司已於 2M26 啟動漲價計

 結論與建議 
隨客製化需求持續成長、柬埔寨廠人力恢復至5,000人並開始
協助客戶回補庫存，帶動3Q26營收創下歷史新高，且受惠於
營收規模擴大、產能利用率提升及關稅返還挹注，淨利率有望
創高。展望後市，公司持續擴增墨西哥產能，員工人數已達
1,800人，後續預計每年增加約500人，以改善交期並滿足客製
化窗簾訂單成長需求；越南新廠預計2027年初投產，第一期規
劃員工約1,000人，可望進一步擴大客製化產品產能與市佔
率。市場需求方面，儘管美國市場面臨高通膨及消費降級壓
力，但公司憑藉穩健的財務結構，客製化產品與同業價差已擴
大至30%～40%，具價格競爭優勢，有助於提升市佔率及整體
訂單量，預期維持mid-single digit成長；固定尺寸則透過新品
推出，支撐營收維持low-single digit成長；歐洲市場方面，隨
荷蘭、比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，可望延續high-single 
digit成長。獲利方面，受惠於關稅稅率降至20%、漲價及營收
規模效益逐步顯現，預期毛利率將延續成長趨勢。綜合以上，
研究部預估2026、2027年EPS為23.92、27.91元，以16倍本
益比評價，給予目標價450元，投資建議為買進。

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 đi ểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 đi ểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có kh ả năng dòng ch ảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi l ựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đ ầu tư công, chính sách ti ền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn). 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

TUẦN 28/09-02/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tu ần qua, -49.02 điểm tương ứng -2.66%, với thanh

[Nhận định xu hướng]

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -49.02 điểm tương ứng -2.66%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Y tế và Bất động sản là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -4,944 tỷ đồng, 
tập trung ở các mã VPB, TCB và VHM; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 1995 
tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng -5,280 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua và đang 
duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế 3,630 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 208 hợp đồng nhưng đang 
duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,428 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ hai liên tiếp sau nâng hạng, khi VN-Index lùi 
về đóng cửa 1.737 điểm — giảm khoảng 2% so với tuần trước và đang kiểm định lại vùng 
đáy cũ của tháng 8, quanh 1.720. Áp lực bán duy trì khi chỉ số giảm nhiều phiên liên tiếp, 
cho thấy hiệu ứng "mua kỳ vọng, bán thực tế" sau nâng hạng vẫn chưa kết thúc, trong bối 
cảnh dòng tiền thận trọng và thanh khoản ở mức thấp. Khối ngoại tiếp tục là lực cản 
chính khi bán ròng mạnh cả 5 phiên trong tuần, riêng phiên 2/10 bán ròng tới 3.361 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở PNJ và HDB. Áp lực bên ngoài vẫn hiện diện khi Fed duy trì lập trường 
diều hâu và giá dầu ở mức cao; trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo KQKD sẽ bắt đầu có 
tác động, kỳ vọng theo chiều hướng tích cực, qua đó giúp nâng đỡ thị trường chung.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên khung tuần, xuyên thủng vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, cho thấy áp lực bán đã có phần quyết liệt hơn sau 2 tuần giằng co trước 
đó. Trên khung ngày, sau các phiên lình xình vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng dần chuyển sang 
diễn biến giảm điểm rõ nét hơn về cuối tuần, đồng thời cắt sâu xuống dưới SMA50, thể 
hiện áp lực phân phối đang có phần lấn át mặc dù chưa xảy ra hiện tượng bán tháo khi 
khối lượng khớp lệnh được giữ ở mức thấp. Diễn biến tính từ vùng đỉnh 1870 vào đầu tháng 
9 đang không mấy khả quan, tuy nhiên xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn, tính từ đáy 1640 vào cuối 
tháng 7, hiện vẫn đang được bảo lưu và VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ có nhịp hồi phục trở lại 
khi về đến vùng hỗ trợ kế tiếp tại quanh 1710. 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1710 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1670 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

### Page 1

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng hóa Sài Gòn(SCS) 
Thách thức từ Long Thành 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS từ 2H2026

Long Thành tạo áp lực lên sản lượng 
và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, triển vọng 
dài hạn phụ thuộc khả năng tham 
gia vận hành nhà ga mới

Cổ tức 2026 dự kiến ở mức 5,000 
VND/cổ phiếu

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang svck). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 tỷ 
VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự suy 
giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng mùa cao điểm hàng hóa cuối năm và mạng bay Trung Đông 
phục hồi sẽ hỗ trợ sản lượng SCS trong những tháng cuối năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ 
cải thiện còn hạn chế do nhu cầu vận tải trên các tuyến Trung Đông – châu Á và 
châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn suy yếu. Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự 
kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy).

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sau khi các hạ tầng kết nối với sân bay Long Thành được hoàn 
thiện, tỷ lệ phân bổ chuyến bay quốc tế giữa Long Thành và Tân Sơn Nhất sẽ dao 
động quanh khoảng 75%/25%. Tỷ trọng phân bổ hàng quốc tế tại TP. Hồ Chí Minh 
qua Long Thành 2027/2028/2029 dự kiến sẽ đạt 10%/35%/55%.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì cổ tức tiền mặt cao nhờ khả năng tạo dòng 
tiền ổn định và quy mô tiền gửi lớn. Cổ tức năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ 
phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027 nhằm tích lũy 
nguồn lực cho các dự án mới.

Dựa trên triển vọng kinh doanh và kết quả định giá, chúng tôi đưa ra khuyến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP với cổ phiếu SCS, mức giá mục tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ hàng hóa Sài Gòn được thành 
lập năm 2008, hiện là đơn vị khai thác nhà ga 
hàng hóa hàng không lớn thứ 2 tại cảng 
hàng không quốc tế (HKQT) Tân Sơn Nhất 
với thị phần khoảng 50%. Các cổ đông lớn 
của SCS bao gồm CTCP Gemadept (33.4%) 
và Tổng Công ty Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam 
(13.6%). 
Hoạt động khai thác nhà ga là mảng kinh 
doanh chính của SCS. Hiện SCS đang tiếp 
tục mua sắm trang thiết bị nâng công suất 
xử lý hàng lên 350,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa năm 2026 dự kiến đạt 209/52 nghìn tấn (-7%/-4% yoy) dù đã được hỗ 
trợ bởi mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi và mùa cao điểm cuối năm 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Thông lượng hàng hóa quốc tế qua SCS bắt đầu giảm từ 2027 với 10%/35%/55%/70% hàng quốc tế 
Tân Sơn Nhất phân bổ sang cho L TA vào 2027/2028/2029/2030, trước khi bắt đầu tăng trưởng trở lại từ 
2031. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định nhờ nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh, cổ tức 2026 
dự kiến đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu và duy trì ở mức 3,000 VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027.

KQKD 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ

2Q2026, SCS ghi nhận doanh thu 279 tỷ VND (-4% yoy), LNST đạt 188 tỷ VND (đi 
ngang yoy). Lũy kế 1H2026, doanh thu đạt 551 tỷ VND (-1% yoy), LNST đạt 361 
tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy), trong đó thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh bù đắp phần lớn sự 
suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga.

Biểu đồ 4. Cơ cấu thông lượng hàng SCS (tấn)  Biểu đồ 5. Giá dịch vụ và biên LNG hoạt động khai thác nhà ga 
của SCS

Nguồn: SCS  Nguồn: SCS

Mạng bay Trung Đông phục hồi hỗ 
trợ sản lượng SCS trong 2H2026, 
song mức độ cải thiện còn hạn chế 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng hàng hóa của SCS sẽ cải thiện trong những tháng 
cuối năm 2026 nhờ mạng bay Trung Đông dần được khôi phục, đặc biệt từ 
Qatar Airways, cùng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa tăng trong mùa cao điểm cuối 
năm. Tuy nhiên, mức độ phục hồi dự kiến còn hạn chế khi nhu cầu vận tải trên 
các tuyến Trung Đông – Châu Á và Châu Âu – Trung Đông vẫn giảm lần lượt 
14.1% và 16.1% yoy trong tháng 7/2026 (tháng thứ 5 liên tiếp), trái ngược với 
mức tăng 3.9% của thị trường hàng hóa hàng không toàn cầu. Diễn biến này 
phần nào giải thích việc sản lượng hàng quốc tế của SCS tiếp tục giảm khoảng 
10% yoy trong tháng 7–8, dù tần suất bay của các hãng Trung Đông đã phục 
hồi đáng kể.

Chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng hàng quốc tế và nội địa trong bốn tháng cuối 
năm lần lượt giảm 4% và 2% yoy, cả năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy) và 
52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy). Doanh thu khai thác nhà ga được kỳ vọng tiếp tục 
được hỗ trợ bởi việc điều chỉnh tăng giá dịch vụ từ đầu năm, bù đắp một phần 
tác động từ sản lượng suy giảm.

Biểu đồ 6. Mức độ phục hồi chuyến bay của các hãng hàng không vùng Vịnh 
Đến ngày 21/09/2026, tần suất bay của các 
hãng vùng Vịnh đã phục hồi đáng kể, phần 
lớn đạt khoảng 85–100% mức trước xung đột, 
riêng Qatar Airways đạt 97%, tăng từ mức 
87% ghi nhận giữa tháng 6.

### Page 5

Long Thành tạo áp lực lên sản 
lượng và biên lợi nhuận từ 2027, 
triển vọng dài hạn phụ thuộc khả 
năng tham gia vận hành nhà ga mới 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhu cầu vận tải hàng hóa hàng không khu vực phía Nam duy 
trì tăng trưởng trong trung hạn, với sản lượng hàng quốc tế/nội địa tăng 8%/4% 
yoy năm 2027 và 9%/6% mỗi năm từ 2028, được hỗ trợ bởi hoạt động xuất 
nhập khẩu, thương mại điện tử và sự phát triển của chuỗi cung ứng. Tuy nhiên, 
sản lượng hàng quốc tế qua SCS dự kiến chịu áp lực khi các chuyến bay từng 
bước chuyển sang Long Thành. Trong kịch bản cơ sở, chúng tôi giả định tỷ lệ 
chuyến bay quốc tế chuyển sang Long Thành lần lượt đạt 10%/35%/55%/70% 
trong giai đoạn 2027–2030, với tác động lên sản lượng thực tế phụ thuộc vào 
tiến độ khai thác nhà ga hàng hóa và hệ thống logistics tại sân bay mới.

Đồng thời, giá dịch vụ quốc tế dự kiến bắt đầu giảm từ giữa năm 2027 nhằm giữ 
chân các hãng hàng không, trong khi giá nội địa kỳ vọng duy trì tăng 3%/năm. 
Theo đó, biên lợi nhuận gộp dự kiến giảm nhẹ trong năm 2027 và chịu áp lực 
đáng kể từ 2028 khi tỷ trọng hàng quốc tế có mức giá dịch vụ cao suy giảm.

Bên cạnh đó, SCS có cơ hội mở rộng quy mô khai thác trong dài hạn thông qua 
việc tham gia vận hành nhà ga hàng hóa số 1 tại Long Thành, công suất thiết kế 
550 nghìn tấn/năm. Doanh nghiệp đã ký biên bản ghi nhớ với ACV về khả năng 
tham gia vận hành nhà ga này, qua đó có thể tiếp cận nguồn hàng dịch chuyển 
từ Tân Sơn Nhất nếu được lựa chọn. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi chưa đưa nguồn thu 
từ dự án vào kịch bản cơ sở khi chưa có kết quả lựa chọn đơn vị vận hành chính 
thức.

Biểu đồ 7. Thông lượng hàng và biên LNG của SCS 2022 – 2028F

Nguồn: SCS, KBSV dự phóng

Nền tảng tài chính lành mạnh và 
chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định 
hỗ trợ định giá cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng SCS tiếp tục duy trì chính sách cổ tức tiền mặt ổn định trong 
những năm tới nhờ khả năng tạo dòng tiền cao, quy mô tiền gửi lớn và mức 
đòn bẩy tài chính thấp. Trong 1H2026, thu nhập tài chính tăng mạnh đã bù đắp 
phần lớn sự suy giảm lợi nhuận từ hoạt động khai thác nhà ga, giúp LNST duy 
trì ổn định ở mức 361 tỷ VND (+0.5% yoy). Chúng tôi dự phóng cổ tức tiền mặt 
năm 2026 tiếp tục đạt 5,000 VND/cổ phiếu, trước khi duy trì ở mức 3,000 
VND/cổ phiếu/năm từ 2027, phù hợp với triển vọng lợi nhuận chịu áp lực từ 
Long Thành và nhu cầu tích lũy nguồn lực tài chính để tham gia các dự án mới.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu  
 Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền (DCF), trên cơ sở cập nhật triển 
vọng kinh doanh và tác động từ quá trình chuyển đổi khai thác sang Long 
Thành, chúng tôi đưa ra giá mục tiêu cho SCS là 50,700 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương với mức tiềm năng sinh lời 14.3% so với mức giá đóng cửa ngày 
22/09/2026. Trong kịch bản cơ sở, chúng tôi chưa đưa nguồn thu từ việc tham 
gia vận hành nhà ga hàng hóa số 1 tại Long Thành vào định giá. Các giả định 
chính bao gồm: 
- Thông lượng hàng quốc tế năm 2026 đạt 209.4 nghìn tấn (-7% yoy), với 
bốn tháng cuối năm giảm 4% yoy do mạng bay Trung Đông chưa phục 
hồi hoàn toàn. Nhu cầu hàng quốc tế khu vực phía nam dự kiến tăng 
8% năm 2027 và 9%/năm trong giai đoạn 2028–2030, kéo theo lượng 
hàng qua SCS trong 2027/2028/2029/2030 sẽ giảm lần lượt -2.8%/-
21%/-25%/-27% yoy, trước khi tăng trưởng trở lại từ 2031 sau khi đạt 
tỷ trọng phân bổ tối ưu giữa SGN và LTA. 
- Thông lượng hàng quốc nội năm 2026 đạt 52.2 nghìn tấn (-4% yoy), với 
bốn tháng cuối năm giảm 2% yoy do áp lực chi phí vận tải và điều 
chỉnh mạng bay. Sản lượng dự kiến phục hồi 4% năm 2027 và 6%/năm 
từ 2028 khi chi phí nhiên liệu hạ nhiệt và năng lực khai thác cải thiện. 
- Giá nội địa dự kiến tăng 3%/năm, trong khi giá quốc tế bắt đầu giảm 
từ giữa 2027 nhằm giữ chân các hãng hàng không. Biên lợi nhuận gộp 
dự kiến giảm nhẹ trong 2027 và chịu áp lực đáng kể từ 2028 khi tỷ 
trọng hàng quốc tế suy giảm và chi phí cố định không giảm tương ứng

với sản lượng.

Document 295: Eurozone_ Improving business sentiment, driven by tech, is spreading to other sectors (2026-10-14)

Status: ok

### Page 1

14 October 2026
The bank
for a changing

EUROZONE: IMPROVING BUSINESS SENTIMENT, DRIVEN BY TECH, IS SPREADING TO OTHER SECTORS
Stéphane Colliac & Tarik Rharrab
*LATEST OBSERVATION: 5 OCTOBER 2026  **ESTIMATE
The September PMI surveys confirm that growth in the Eurozone is getting stronger, boosted in particular by new export orders, which are
still rising. Sectors that were already performing well – technology and capital goods (including for defence) – are continuing to grow, while

those that had been struggling until now – aeronautics, consumer goods and financial services – are seeing their production indices recover.
This strong performance is particularly noteworthy, given that it has not yet been undermined by higher inflation.
The heatmap shows the PMI index readings: a reading above 50 indicates expansion, while a reading below 50 signals contraction compared to the previous
month. For each indicator, sectors are ranked in descending order based on their September 2026 reading.

Shades of red represent values above 50, while shades of blue represent values below 50: the intensity of the colour increases the further away they are
from this threshold.
Production growth spreads to more sectors
The PMI surveys on business sentiment confirm that business activity in the Eurozone is expanding at a faster pace and spreading to more sectors.
Household-consumption-related sectors (goods and services), which were adversely affected in the spring by the outbreak of the conflict in the Middle

East, do not appear to be negatively impacted by the rising inflation observed since August. The aeronautics sector, where production has mainly been
limited by supply constraints, is also showing signs of improvement.
SOURCE: S&P GLOBAL, BNP PARIBAS
Eurozone sectoral PMI indices: production and new export orders are strengthening across more sectors

Chart of the Week / 14 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
These trends, although still moderate, come alongside a drive to upgrade equipment, fuelled by investment in digital technology, electrification
(which is supporting the automotive sector) and defence. The momentum in technology goods and services further strengthened in September.
The production index for computer and electronics hit a five-year high, while the software services production index stayed close to its July peak.

Financial services are also enjoying a significant improvement, reflecting the healthy lending momentum despite interest rates starting to rise
(see our analysis).
Growth driven by exports
Exports drove growth in the first half of 2026, both across the Eurozone as a whole (see our analysis) and among its key members (notably
Germany). This momentum is also increasing, as indicated by the ‘new export orders’ component of the various PMI indices. As was the case with

production, there has been a particularly noteworthy increase for capital goods (technology and machinery) and related services, particularly digital
services. This trend is playing out more broadly, as, in September, new export orders rose across all sub-sectors, with the exception of intermediate
goods and consumer goods, where contraction recently eased. There has been a significant improvement in the transport sector, particularly in
the aeronautics industry, which has major markets in Asia. Recent data on French exports show that aeronautics exports to this region are rising

once again.
The convergence between rising production and increasing export-order backlogs shows that Eurozone growth is based on an increasingly solid
foundation. Inflationary pressures are not holding it back for the time being.

France has also seen its debt rise in relative terms. As recently as the GFC in 2008, France’s
debt-to-GDP was basically in line with Germany’s and well below Italy’s, but today it’s far
above Germany’s and closing in on Italy’s. Outside of the temporary Covid spike, Italian
debt/GDP has been stable for over a decade.

France now has

yields of the four
Back in the Euro Crisis of the early 2010s, markets priced France closer to Germany than to
Italy and Spain, but today that’s turned around and France now has the highest yields of all
four.

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank

Senior US Economist

October 23, 2026 
A client-facing version of this report is available at the Deutsche Bank Research 
Portal. The following Deutsche Bank Research analysts contributed to this report: 
Brett Ryan (econ), Binky Chadha and Parag Thatte (equity strategy), Benjamin 
Soff (data centers), Daniel Knauff (software), George Hill (healthcare), Omotayo 
Okusanya (REITs), Peter Abramowitz (office REITs), Mark DeVries (consumer and 
specialty finance), Nicole DeBlase (MI/EE), Corinne Blanchard (clean tech), 
Richa Harnain (transports), and Scott Deuschle (aerospace and defense).  
With the 2026 US midterm elections approaching, we look at key investor 
debates surrounding the event and legislative priorities of the next Congress. 
Despite the uncertainty that midterms can bring, the market appears fairly 
confident on expectations for the overall results. While a surprise Republican-
majority victory in both chambers is still possible, consensus is converging 
around current polling indicating Democrats are likely to flip the seats needed for 
a House majority and may win a Senate majority. Historically, an incumbent 
president has lost seats in 20 of the last 22 midterms. Investor expectations are 
in line with this. Polymarket, for example, is currently above 90% for Democrats 
gaining control of the House and above 60% for Democrats gaining control of the 
Senate.  
With this set of expectations, key investor debates instead focus on the 
legislative agenda for hot-ticket policy issues. Namely, these hinge on AI and 
data centers, affordability (affecting healthcare, housing, and education loans), 
and economic and fiscal policy. Debates also surround possible influence of the 
new Congress on the war in Iran and, by extension, energy prices. In this report, 
we cut through the noise on what the next Congress could reasonably advance 
from these agendas, what the impact would be on affected sectors, and how to 
address market uncertainty – and perceived certainty – into the elections. 
Historically, midterm elections have proven to be a check on administration 
policies. The number of seats lost in the midterms by the incumbent party is tied 
to the president’s approval rating going into midterms, which in turn tends to 
align with consumer confidence. President Trump’s ~38% approval rating, at a 
record low for his tenure, would suggest a little over a 45-seat loss for the 
Republican Party (in the House and Senate combined). That said, polling in the 
Senate races that will determine control of the chamber largely remains within 
the margin of error, and in 2024, Trump won in at least five of the seven core toss-
up states. Thus, the Senate remains a much closer call than the House race.

### Page 2

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 2

Figure 1: Presidential Approval vs. House Seats

Source: Real Clear Politics

Figure 2: Presidential Approval vs. Consumer Confidence

Source: The Conference Board

Whatever the outcome of the  midterms, the legislative agenda will 
still face constraints from the White House. It is all but impossible for either party 
to gain a veto -proof majority  in either chamber, minimizing the likelihood of a 
large shift in fiscal or regulatory policy. The most  immediately obvious impact of 
Democrats taking the Senate would be the ability to slow or block executive 
branch appointees, including at the Federal Reserve Board of Governors or the 
Supreme Court, should any members step down before their terms end.

### Page 3

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 3

Without garnering a two-thirds majority in both chambers, Democrats lack a clear 
path to unwind the 2025 tax cuts or enact AI-related regulatory legislation – at 
least, not without President Trump’s explicit approval. With respect to the near -
term fiscal policy outlook, the most important period for investors may be the 
“lame duck” session, the period after the November 3 election and before the new 
Congress convenes on January 3. Recall that current federal government funding 
expires on December 11, and the Trump administration is asking for a nearly 60% 
increase in defense spending, the largest since the Korean War.  In addition, the 
debt limit will become binding again around the middle of next year. In short, the 
actions during the lame duck session could determine whether the new Congress 
is seated amid a government shutdown, a contentious battle over the federal 
budget, and the debt limit looming in the background.  
In terms of the market reaction to midterm  elections, the risk-reward profile has 
typically been favorable , with 21 of the last 23 midterm years  seeing positive 
returns in the fourth quarter, with an average gain of 7% in the S&P. The gains are 
notable even after adjusting for typical positive year-end seasonality.

Figure 3: S&P Returns and Midterms

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP

It w ould not be unusual for the market to go sideways as investors hedge 
potential volatility and uncertainty, as we have seen previously ahead of other 
well-telegraphed pivotal events. This has historically been followed by a rally as 
the results became clear and  uncertainty dissipated, regardless of the result 
itself. In the current context, if election polls and prediction market probabilities 
continue being lopsided, the market may not treat the election as an uncertain 
risk event going in. Uncertainty could still ramp up after, and the typical midterm 
election rally may be pushed out. It is notable that the one prominent exception 
to the midterm rallies was in 2018 , when the market sold off sharply after the 
elections as uncertainty spiked around trade war 1.0 as well as Fed policy.

### Page 4

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 4

We note that the midterm elections also coincide with the third quarter earnings 
season, during which we expect companies to deliver growth of 34%. For next 
year, we expect growth to be a still -robust 17% with risks to the upside. 
Eventually, earnings tend to be the anchor for equities and have been for several 
years despite numerous domestic political as well as geopolitical shocks. 
Across sectors, most have rallied around past midterms in keeping with the 
market, but there isn’t a consistent pattern of out - or underperformance . This 
suggests that for the initial conditions for each sector, specific policies and 
positioning played a bigger role.  For that reason, we turn to a closer analysis of 
key legislative and policy debates impacting exposed sectors.

### Page 5

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 5

AI and Data Centers  
AI and data centers have become a campaign talking point for candidates from 
both the Democratic and Republican parties. This has spurred investor questions 
on whether to expect a national regulatory framework constraining data center 
buildouts. Some of th e headlines surrounding this debate overestimate what 
Congress can  accomplish through national legislation compared with what is 
possible at a state level, particularly during a presidential administration 
supportive of AI and data center acceleration. Still, the industry is responding to 
a political backdrop that is increasingly scrutinizing AI and data centers.  
The current administration has prioritized advancing AI and digital infrastructure 
development. The White House ’s AI Action Plan , released July 2025, included a 
set of executive orders supporting the rapid buildout of AI data centers by 
reducing certain federal regulatory and permitting burdens. The administration 
did not preempt state laws on data center infrastructure development and 
federal permitting orders generally do not override state zoning and utility rules. 
In March 2026 , the White House announced the Ratepayer Protection Pledge, 
under which large data center operators would voluntarily commit to covering 
the cost to upgrade generation, transmission, substations, or other necessary 
infrastructure to service these developments.  The industry’s response to these 
proposals has been largely constructive. The Data Center Coalition  (DCC), an 
industry group representing major  hyperscalers and data center  owners and 
operators came out in support of the initial version of the Pledge, while arguing 
that large -load customers across all industries also should cover the costs to 
upgrade energy infrastructure. According to the DCC , “the data center 
industry remains committed to being responsible partners where we  operate, 
which includes  paying our full energy costs  and investing in local jobs and 
workforce development.” 
Congress voted in September 2026 to codify the principles present in the Pledge 
via the Ratepayer Protection Act. The bill, which only required states to “consider” 
these standards, passed overwhelmingly in the House (417-3), but failed to reach 
60 votes in the Senate (57 -43). Critics noted the bill stopped short of imposing 
mandatory nationwide rules. 
Looking forward, we expect a Congress that is more focused on consumer 
protection might look to codify a more stringent version of these rules, requiring 
large-load customers to bear the full incremental costs associated with 
generation, transmission, and distribution upgrades.  However, even if such a 
framework were passed into law, we believe the overall economic impact to large 
data center operators would be relatively modest, for two reasons. 
For one, the White House would maintain a veto over  bills that include language 
it views as overly burdensome towards AI infrastructure development . Two, data 
center operators  generally underwrite projects only when they have a high 
degree of confidence in securing power and, where necessary, may already be 
embedding the cost of upgrading energy infrastructure into their investment 
plans. We expect developers to be capable of passing on sustained increases in 
power infrastructure costs to their customers , which, for many 100  MW+ 
developments, are the major investment-grade hyperscalers.

### Page 6

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 6

Though data center development is receiving increasing national attention, it is 
still largely a local issue, and the most consequential elections will be at the state 
and local levels. We have seen a number of state -driven actions on data center s 
in recent months, including: 
• In July, New York State’s governor issued an executive order to establish 
a temporary moratorium on data center development  while the state 
develops a standardized framework of rules for key issues (including 
responsibilities for power and energy infrastructure upgrades; additional 
power generation; clean energy; water use, emission, and waste 
management; and state/local financ ial incentives). The New York 
legislature also passed a one -year moratorium on Department of 
Environmental Conservation permits and approvals for 20  MW+ data 
center developments. 
• In August, Texas’ governor directed the state’s public utility commission 
to conduct a comprehensive audit of every data center advancing 
through the ERCOT (Texas’s grid operator) interconnection process. The 
audit will cover several notable areas including public financial support, 
electricity demand and supply, water use, community impacts, and 
ownership and control. 
• In September, California’s governor passed a comprehensive regulatory 
package, covering electricity, water, reporting, and environmental 
review. Most of these provisions take effect January 1, 2027, and phase 
in through 2027 -2028, but the package does not ban data centers or 
impose a statewide moratorium on development activity. 
• Oregon, Pennsylvania, New Jersey, and other states have also 
established orders to create standards and frameworks governing data 
center development (but without imposing broad moratoriums). 
At the more local level, a large number of moratoriums have been passed on new 
data center development. Data collected by SemiAnalysis, an independent 
research firm, shows that, as of September, more than 300 local data  center 
moratoriums have been adopted nationwide across counties, municipalities, 
townships, and other local governments.  
However, these state and local moratoriums appear  thus far to be less impactful 
to near- and medium-term development activity than headline coverage might 
otherwise suggest. According to SemiAnalysis, only about 2.3 GW of the 38 GW 
of data center capacity planned or under construction for 2027 has faced delays 
as a result of  the New York state executive order (0.8 GW) and/or local 
moratoriums (1.5 GW). 
We believe the primary reasons that moratoriums are, in reality, less likely to 
impact near -term development activity are (1) that these moratoriums largely 
impact new projects and applications (rather than existing developments in  
progress), (2) there may be flexibility to relocate or rezone early-stage projects in 
response to moratoriums, and (3) data center developers are increasingly 
partnering with local communities to gain their support via investments and other 
programs (as we wrote about recently).  Potential regulatory risk has not yet 
slowed capex spending patterns, with capex spend still expected to climb from

### Page 7

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 7

$0.5 trillion in 2025 to $1.3 trillion by 2027; data centers typically represent ~20-
30% of the total bill of materials for new AI capacity.  
Although the practical impact on near- and medium-term development pipelines 
appears less pronounced than headlines might suggest, all this is not to say that 
data centers will not face increasing local opposition following the midterms. 
Indeed, i n our conversations with industry participants, there is an 
acknowledgment that greater public scrutiny of data centers is not going away.  
Additional moratoriums, zoning restrictions, permitting requirements, or utility -
related conditions could affect the pace, cost , and overall feasibility of future 
projects.

### Page 8

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 8

Affordability Agenda: Healthcare, Housing, and 
Education Loans  
The affordability agenda has become a centerpiece of many Democratic as well 
as Republican campaign platforms.  This platform is impacting investor 
debates across healthcare, housing, and student loans. In healthcare,  the push 
among some Democrats for expanded health coverage under the Medicare for All 
banner is placing pressure on managed care stocks, but meanwhile, companies 
with exposure to Medicaid and  the Affordable Care Act (ACA) could see a 
supportive political backdrop. On housing, investor debates on the impacts of the 
affordability agenda have continued since passage of legislation in July 2026 that 
caps the number of single -family homes for -profit institutional investors can 
purchase. Affordability of higher education is likewise part of the conversatio n, 
leading investors to debate which levers Congress could pull that would affect 
student loan companies.  
Healthcare 
In healthcare, w e see the upcoming midterm elections as a sentiment event for 
managed care stocks , not a policy event.  With the presidential veto in place 
through 2028, we do not expect the government to enact direct healthcare 
legislation during the next Congress. Forecasts place the probability of a change 
in House control at roughly 79%, with both chambers changing in about 44% of 
simulations. Although investor skittishness around Medicare for All (M4A) rhetoric 
is high, we see the likelihood of M4A replacing private coverage as a near -zero 
probability in this timeframe.  
M4A has no realistic legislative path before 2029, but the rhetoric weighs on 
managed care organization (MCO) stock multiples by raising perceived long-term 
risk rather than affecting near -term earnings. Polling from  Kaiser Family 
Foundation, a health policy research organization, shows support for M4A at a 
decade-plus high, and the 2018 –2019 cycle suggests that increased campaign 
attention can pressure MCO multiples even without a direct policy impact. We 
expect similar sentiment -driven pressure through year -end and potentially into 
the 2028 primary cycle but would not view it as a change i n near -term 
fundamentals.  
For Medicare Advantage, we see a h eadline risk over earnings reality. The near-
term earnings impact for affected companies should be limited, because the US 
Centers for Medicare & Medicaid Services (CMS) has finalized a 2.48% Medicare 
Advantage (MA) rate increase for 2027, versus 0.09% in the advance notice. A 
change in House control would instead increase focus on 2028 payment policy, 
risk adj ustment, and prior authorization. Coding reform already has bipartisan 
support: sponsors estimate that the Cassidy -Merkley No UPCODE Act could 
generate up to $275  billion of savings over ten years, while MedPAC estimates 
coding intensity will add roughly $40  billion to 2025 Medicare Advantage  costs 
relative to traditional Medicare. In our view, the more immediate consideration is 
potential multiple pressure rather than a measurable change to company 
earnings.  
However, the next Congress could b enefit Medicaid funding and ACA policy. 
Medicaid and the ACA appear relatively better positioned, although any benefit

### Page 9

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 9

would likely be reflected first in sentiment and lower perceived policy risk rather 
than near -term estimate revisions. Medicaid community engagement 
requirements take effect in most states by January 1, 2027. Although repeal 
remains unlikely given the presidential veto, a change in House control could shift 
the year -end funding debate toward  restoring or  preserving federal Medicaid 
support, which could ease state budget pressure and support rate -setting. The 
ACA policy backdrop could also become more supportive, although we see 
limited scope for enhanced premium subsidies to return before 2029 after the 
three-year extension passed by the House stalled.  
Housing 
Turning to the housing sector, the likely midterm outcome of a split government 
would result in more gridlock. Historically, political gridlock in terms of passing 
new regulation has been a tailwind for the real estate sector, reducing regulatory 
risk at the federal level. In the scenario of a split Congress, in which the Democrats 
have a majority in the House but not the Senate, bills with bipartisan support have 
a greater likelihood to become law. Two key areas in which this bipartisan support 
exists are housing affordability and consumer protection. 
Among real estate investment trusts ( REITs), we see Residential as among the 
most impacted subsectors, particularly as it relates to the affordability debate. 
Regardless of the election outcome, housing affordability has become front and 
center on the political agenda  across both parties. F ederal legislation  is more 
likely to target supply -side solutions, as legislation restricting rent increases is 
typically handled at the local and state level. We think Multifamily would be the 
most likely subsector impacted, as a push to expand rental housing supply could 
lead to eased zoning restrictions, particularly in supply -constrained coastal 
markets that have historically benefited from higher barriers to entry. That said, 
this would likely take years to materialize. With a Democratic majority , Congress 
could voice opposition to the influence of large institutional landlords, but we see 
this strictly as a sentiment risk to stocks.  
Single Family For Rent (SFR) is less exposed to near -term legislative shocks, as 
the sector was a focus of the 21st Century ROAD to Housing Act passed in July 
2026 over the summer. The  measure impacts institutional owners’ ability to buy 
homes off the MLS but contains specific carve-out provisions that will allow them 
to keep developing build -to-rent (BTR) product and buy excess inventory from 
homebuilders, the sector's preferred acquis ition channel in recent years. The 
ROAD to Housing Act also contained provisions that could theoretically ease the 
production of manufactured homes, the main business segment for 
Manufactured Housing (MH) REITs. The first-order impact is likely more flexibility 
in home designs that could lead to further densification of MH communities, 
although we note emerging investor debate about the long -term impacts of 
increased MH production on competitive supply for a property type t hat has 
historically been supply constrained, mainly due to NIMBY-ism.  
Parallel to the conversation on affordability, it is clear that, regardless of which 
political party may be in power following the midterms, a major focus remains 
addressing the US fiscal deficit and its continued growth. We continue to pay 
attention to potential budget cuts or sequestration to slow government spending 
and reduce the deficit, which could have an impact on real estate.  Key areas to 
watch include cuts to healthcare spending (which would be a negative for skilled

### Page 10

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 10

nursing focused REITs, given Medicare is a major revenue source for their tenant 
base) and cuts that would impact Office REITs with large government exposure.  
We also worry that an elevated federal deficit and the elevated Treasury 
borrowing needs today create a risk that interest rates will remain  higher for 
longer. This puts pressure on real estate valuations and cost of financing for REITs 
to drive external growth via acquisitions and development. A split Congress is 
likely to result in more difficult budget negotiations and increased oversight, but 
it remains unclear w hat the ultimate outcome would be to address the budget 
deficit issue. 
With real estate being more dependent on local market dynamics, we believe the 
more likely regulatory impact would come from local regulation. This could show 
up on the ballots during the midterms , with increased possibility of rent control 
legislation targeted at residential real estate as well as self -storage, as cities and 
municipalities focus on affordability issues. There is also growing legislative 
pushback at the local level against development of real assets such as data 
centers given the large power requirements, as discussed in detail elsewhere in 
this report.  
Education Lending 
Midterm campaign platforms on education affordability  have a bearing on the 
consumer and specialty finance sector. Much of the policy exposure in this sector 
is through the executive branch bodies, not Congress, however. This includes, for 
example, regulatory authorities  such as the FDIC (Federal Deposit Insurance 
Corporation), the CFPB (Consumer Financial Protection Bureau ), or the FHFA 
(Federal Housing Finance Agency, which regulates Fannie Mae and Freddie 
Mac). That said, historical data shows that student loan company stocks can still 
directionally align with midterm party outcome and expectations  in Congress, 
although the sample size is small.

### Page 11

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 11

Mixed Impacts of Economic and Fiscal Policy  
Democratic and Republican candidates have aimed to distinguish themselves 
from their opponents along a range of economic and fiscal policy matters, 
including tariffs, tax, labor, and the budget. In reality, the outcome of the 
midterms, regardless of which party prevails, is likely to present a mixture of relief 
and restrictions affecting businesses. In some cases, investor fears over sweeping 
economic policy changes are overblown for topics over which Congress 
realistically has limited influence, presenting more headline  risk than a risk to 
fundamentals. In considering these, we look to industrials that are particularly 
sensitive to key investor debates , spanning multi-industry/electrical equipment, 
clean tech and alternative energy, transport, and defense.  
For the multi-industry and electrical equipment (MI/EE) sector, historical midterm 
election cycles suggest a generally constructive backdrop heading into election 
day, with positive returns in six of the past seven elections. On average, the MI/EE 
group has gained ~5% during the three months preceding historical midterm 
elections and ~8% during the final month prior. Relative outperformance v ersus 
the S&P 500 has been less consistent; we observed outperformance in only four 
of seven cycles, though the group still generated average excess returns of 
~250bps over the preceding three months and ~270bps over the preceding 
month.  
Overall, the data supports a favorable absolute return backdrop ahead of 
midterms, but significant stock -level dispersion suggests company -specific 
fundamentals and factor leadership have historically mattered more than the 
election cycle alone. We mapped out election results scenarios across key policy 
pillars for our coverage (tax policy and capital investment; trade, tariffs, and 
supply chains; energy, grid, and power infrastructure; AI and data center 
regulation; and the regulatory environment). We focus on two key scenarios: one 
in which Republicans maintain control of both chambers, and a divided 
government that sees the Democrats controlling one or both chambers of the 
legislative branch.  
Under a divided government, we expect stock -level dispersion would likely 
narrow, with globally exposed companies receiving some trade policy relief and 
HVAC/automation companies benefiting from a potentially more supportive 
regulatory backdrop. However, should Republicans maintain control of Congress, 
our scenario analysis suggests this would be favorable for electrical equipment 
and power infrastructure manufacturers, due to a combination of pro-investment 
tax policy, faster infrastructure deployment, and a lighter AI regulatory 
framework. That said, globally exposed companies could face incremental tariff 
and retaliation risks.  
Looking to the clean tech and alternative energy sector, the midterm elections 
could represent a positive tailwind . T he next Congress might be positioned to 
pursue targeted extensions or modifications to certain clean -energy tax credits. 
The introduction of H.R. 8477, which proposes to restore several clean energy tax 
credits rolled back in late 2025, seems to signal tha t some, albeit a limited 
number, of Republicans could be supportive of this initiative. If the Democrats 
gain a majority in the House but not the Senate in the midterms, it is plausible that 
Democrats could find sufficient  Republican support to advance

### Page 12

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 12

legislation extending clean -energy credits, though they would still face 
significant legislative hurdles. A midterm outcome in which Republicans retain 
control of both chambers is likely to maintain the status quo  for the remaining 
existing tax credits, especially for those tied to domestic manufacturing. While 
those could potentially see further extension, we think this is a limited outcome.    
While we see a low probability of a major change in, or support for, the residential 
tax credits that expired at the end of 2025 (25D), we envision a scenario in which 
indirect residential tax credits (48E) could be extended, which would help boost 
part of the demand. Softness  in demand is expected to remain going into 2027, 
still impacted by the phaseout of the 25D tax credits, the eventual roll-off of safe-
harbor benefits, and a more constrained financing environment.  
From a manufacturing tax credit standpoint,  there could be additional tailwinds  
if we were to see any manufacturing tax credit extension or positive modification 
(although lower probability, in our view) . This would also likely improve utility -
scale project economics if broader project -related credits are preserved, though 
upcoming elections could cause a slowdown in new booking activity until 
legislative uncertainty clears.  
For Transports,  the midterms would be most relevant for the trucking supply 
thesis. Truckload stocks could have a modest negative reaction to a Democratic 
majority if investors expect some rollback of the Trump administration’s 
constraints on drivers , which has tightened supply . We would separate 
perception from fundamentals, however. The election of a Democratic Congress 
would not automatically by itself change leadership at the Department of 
Transportation (DOT) and the Federal Motor Carrier Safety Administration 
(FMCSA), the executive branch entities driving much of the current push  for 
restrictions on drivers.  
One possible impact of  the midterms for  Transports is if  a change in 
Congressional control  contributed to a faster resolution of the war in Iran . Any 
resulting lower energy prices would be a positive for Transports . We view this as 
a lower -probability outcome,  however, given the constraints on a divided 
government.  
With clashes over the budget likely looming, aerospace and def ense could get 
caught in the crosshairs. W hile defense spending has historically enjoyed 
bipartisan support, it has grown politicized following the war in Iran, making it 
more difficult for a split Congress to pass such a budget. The defense budget has 
experienced significant growth over the last few years, rising from $900 billion in 
FY2024 to $1 trillion in FY2026. The Trump administration would like to see this 
trend accelerate: in April 2026, the president issued a budget request that called 
for a $1.5  trillion budget for the Department of Defense in FY 2027. With a 
continuing resolution likely into year-end, the outcome of the midterm elections 
will determine whether the next Congress can pass such a budget. If Republicans 
hold the House and Senate, we think there  is a reasonable prospect of a budget 
of $1.2 trillion or more  passing into law . Defense investors would see that as a 
substantial tailwind for beaten-up defense stocks. On the other hand, this would 
be unlikely in a split Congress, in our view.  Given all this, we see the outcome of 
the upcoming midterm elections as key to determining how US defense stocks 
will perform into year end and beyond.

### Page 13

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 13

Taking Stock of the Known Unknowns 
Although Republicans could maintain control of both chambers,  consensus is 
converging around polls pointing to a Democratic majority in the House  and 
possibly the Senate. The perceived certainty has allowed the investor debate 
related to midterms to focus not only on who will win but how they will govern. In 
some cases, headlines are misrepresenting what a Congress constrained by the 
executive will be able to accomplish . In other areas, legislation that is expected 
to be passed will need to be taken together with offsetting factors. Whatever the 
outcome, the political backdrop will continue to influence the market and 
investor debates in the year ahead.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
In November 2025, we introduced Westpac-Now (see here),
a continuously-updated measure of current activity in the
Australian economy. Westpac-Now draws on more than
60 high frequency economic and financial variables, using
advanced statistical techniques to provide a timely pulse

check on economic momentum. It is the first Australian
model to incorporate internal banking data, enhancing its
predictive power.
This note provides the second estimate of GDP growth for the
September quarter 2026, using domestic and international
data released up to and including the September Labour Force
Survey (released 15 October 2026). Updates are published
monthly (see Appendix A for the release schedule).
Westpac Monthly Activity Index: activity falters as

headwinds mount
After showing tentative signs of improvement through the
second half of 2025, the Westpac Monthly Activity Index has
weakened markedly over 2026. The Index moved back into
negative territory in February and has declined further since.
While there were some signs of stabilisation around mid-year,
this proved to be short lived with the Index falling to –0.88 in
September, outside of the pandemic period, the weakest read
since 2013.
The economy is coming under pressure from multiple

fronts, with key headwinds coming from higher interest
rates, renewed disruptions to global energy markets and a
deepening in the housing downturn. Among households,
spending held up surprisingly well through the first half of the
year despite historically weak sentiment, but more recently
Growth momentum on shaky ground
spending outside of fuel has started to cool (see here). At
the same time, the labour market has continued to soften,
with unemployment reaching a cycle high of 4.7% (see here).

Meanwhile, the business sector is also showing signs of
softening, with sentiment deteriorating and conditions holding
at weak levels (see here). Business credit growth remains
a notable exception, although even here there are tentative
signs of easing (see here).
This deterioration is particularly evident in our heatmap.
Negative contributions have become both broader and more
pronounced through 2026, with the weakness intensifying
in August and September. Labour and business condition

indicators are now the largest sources of weakness, while
spending and confidence have also deteriorated materially.
Credit and capacity utilisation indicators have been
comparatively less negative, but there are currently few areas
providing a meaningful offset to the broader softening.
Our indicator heatmap decomposes changes in the Monthly
Activity Index into group-level contributions, allowing us to
assess which components are driving movements in activity.

For ease of interpretation, indicators are grouped into broad
categories (see the Appendix for further details). For example,
the labour market category includes both official ABS Labour
Force Survey outcomes and timelier survey-based measures of
labour demand and job advertisements.
Westpac-Now: Resilient growth outlook slowing not
stalling
Recall that Westpac-Now is a refined indicator that combines
our Monthly Activity Index with advanced econometric

techniques to estimate GDP growth. As previously noted,
the fit is not expected to be exact. Measured GDP includes
Neha Sharma
Economist

### Page 3

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
components that are not well captured by high-frequency
activity indicators, such as imputed rents for owner-occupiers,
housing-related ownership transfer costs, public and business
inventories, and depreciation.
To bridge monthly and quarterly data, we use a MIDAS

framework, allowing us to combine mixed-frequency inputs
and incorporate partial information for the current quarter
when producing GDP forecasts.
Based on current model estimates, Westpac-Now’s second
estimate for Q3 2026 points to GDP growth of 0.5%qtr
(with a 70% confidence interval of –0.1%qtr to 1.0%qtr)
and 2.1%yr, representing a slight downgrade from the first
estimate of +0.6%qtr but still a step up from the 0.4%qtr
(2.1%yr) outcome recorded in Q2 2026. While aggregate

growth appears to have held up through Q3, the underlying
picture became softer as the quarter progressed. At the
second decimal place the estimate is 0.46%qtr, leaving it
close to rounding down to 0.4%qtr.
With several headwinds now bearing on the economy
simultaneously, there is a growing risk that the slowdown in
activity becomes more pronounced than currently anticipated.
At the same time, the strength of the business investment
upswing provides an important counterweight that should

help cushion the broader slowdown.
Looking ahead, model dynamics suggest growth will tick
down to 0.4%qtr in Q4 2026 before picking up in Q1 2027.
However, these projections remain much more tentative,
given their reliance on projected inputs rather than realised
data. In addition, heightened uncertainty, particularly around
the renewed tensions in the Middle East conflict, means risks
around these forward estimates are larger than usual.
September Quarter data: a softer end to the quarter

Data released in the first month of the September quarter
indicated that the economy has picked up momentum relative
to the previous quarter, with our first nowcast estimating Q3
growth at 0.6%qtr, in line with the 0.6%qtr “pure forecast”
based solely on data available up to June 2026.
However, the flow of data since then shows downside risks to
growth have begun to materialise. Incorporating the August,
followed by the September (to date), data both yielded

nowcasts of 0.5%qtr (with a slight softening at the second
decimal place). This contrasts with Q2 2026, when weakness
emerged early in the quarter, in April, before subsequent data
pointed to stabilisation.
Note that monthly activity estimates are more uncertain
than normal when inflation is shifting. Faster price growth
can cause nominal indicators, such as consumer spending,
to rise more strongly even though ‘real’, inflation-adjusted
spending may be softening (and vice versa). The inclusion

of ‘real’ indicators, such as employment and banking
transaction volumes, helps mitigate some – but not all – of this
uncertainty.
Bottom line:
Westpac-Now points to GDP growth of around 0.5%qtr
(2.1%yr) in Q3 2026, suggesting aggregate growth held up
reasonably well through the quarter, although the flow of
data points to softer momentum as the quarter progressed.
The substantial upswing in business investment remains
an important source of support, helping to offset mounting

weakness elsewhere in the economy.
Looking ahead, the accumulation of headwinds is expected to
see growth slow further towards year-end, with Westpac-Now
currently pointing to growth of around 0.4%qtr in Q4 2026.
Downside risks around the outlook have increased, but for now
our central case remains one of slower growth rather than a
material downturn.

Appendix A: Westpac-Now Release Schedule (2026)
Going forward, Westpac Now will be published on the Friday following each ABS Labour Force Survey (LFS) release, at 11:30am AEDT/AEST (Canberra time).
Each release incorporates the latest labour market data alongside other high frequency indicators and provides an updated nowcast for quarterly GDP growth.

As the quarter progresses, each update should be interpreted as a progressively more complete estimate of that quarter’s economic performance.

Document 526: 企業物価は鈍化も、上昇圧力はなお強い（26年9月企業物価指数） ～川下への価格転嫁が続く。資源高による再加速リスクも～

Source PDF: all report pdf/企業物価は鈍化も、上昇圧力はなお強い（26年9月企業物価指数） ～川下への価格転嫁が続く。資源高による再加速リスクも～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-13

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年10 月 13 日

1/4 
企業物価は鈍化も、上昇圧力はなお強い（26 年 9 月企業物価指数） 
～川下への価格転嫁が続く。資源高による再加速リスクも～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ ９月の国内企業物価指数は前月比▲0.3％、前年比＋6.8％（８月：前年比＋7.6％）。 前年比
上昇率は４ヵ月ぶりに＋７％を下回ったが、農林水産物や石油製品の下落による影響が大き
く、企業段階の物価上昇圧力はなお根強い。 
○ 川上・川中では価格上昇に一服感がみられる一方、川下のプラスチック製品では高い伸びが
続いている。既往のコスト高の価格転嫁はなお進んでおり、川下への物価上昇圧力は依然と
して強い。 
○ ＡＩ関連需要の拡大を背景に、半導体や銅などの価格上昇も目立つ。資源価格とは異なる経
路からの物価上昇圧力として注目される。 
○ 先行きは、川下への価格転嫁が続くなか、資源価格の高止まりによって川上からの上昇圧力
が再び強まるリスクがある。企業物価は当面高止まりし、消費者物価についても年度後半に
かけて上昇率を明確に高める可能性が高い。

1. 前年比は＋６％台に鈍化も、物価上昇圧力はなお根強い 
日本銀行から公表された2026 年９月の国内企業物価指数は、前月比▲0.3％、前年比＋6.8％と
なった。前月比では２ヵ月連続で下落し、前年比でも４ヵ月ぶりに＋７％を下回った。８月の前
年比＋7.6％から大きく鈍化しており、春先の急激な物価上昇には一服感がみられる。 
もっとも、企業段階での物価上昇圧力が大きく和らいだわけではない。今月は農林水産物や石
油・石炭製品の下落が全体の前月比を押し下げた一方、プラスチック製品など川下への価格転嫁
は続き、ＡＩ関連の価格上昇も目立った。また、足元の資源価格の高止まりを踏まえると、先行
きは川上からの上昇圧力が再び強まるリスクもあることにも注意が必要だ。 
前月比の内訳をみると、農林水産物が同▲8.8％（寄与度：▲0.42％ポイント）と大幅に下落し
た。玄米や精米、豚肉などが主な下落要因となった。米については、2026 年産米の概算金の引き
下げなどを背景に価格が大きく低下したとみられる。農林水産物は前年比でも▲13.3％と大幅な
マイナスとなっており、９月の企業物価を押し下げる大きな要因となった。

### Page 2

2/4 
また、石油・石炭製品も前月比▲2.2％と下落した（寄与度：▲0.15％ポイント） 。重油、軽
油、ジェット燃料油などの下落が影響している。農林水産物と石油・石炭製品のマイナス寄与は
合計▲0.57％ポイントと、国内企業物価全体の前月比▲0.3％を大きく上回っている。この２つを
除けばプラス寄与となっており、企業物価の下落が一部に集中していたことが分かる。 
実際、プラスチック製品が前月比＋1.1％、金属製品が同＋0.7％、化学製品が同＋0.3％、飲食
料品が同＋0.2％、電子部品・デバイスが同＋0.4％となるなど、多くの工業製品で価格上昇がみ
られた。また、報道によれば、日銀が公表する515 品目のうち、前年比で価格が上昇した品目は
460 品目と、全体の約９割に達している。国内企業物価の前年比上昇率は鈍化したものの、値上が
りは依然として幅広い品目に及んでいる。企業段階での物価上昇圧力はなお根強いとみるべきだ
ろう。

2. 川中価格は下落も、川下への価格転嫁は続く 
石油化学関連では、春先にみられた急激な価格上昇の反動が続いている。石油・石炭製品は前
月比▲2.2％と、３ヵ月連続で下落した。ベンゼンが同＋17.2％、キシレンが同＋6.0％と、川上
の石油化学系基礎製品の一部で上昇もみられるが、ベンゼンについては中国の製油所の定期修理
に伴う供給減少という個別要因も影響しているようだ。川上価格全体でみれば、このところやや
落ち着きもみられる。 
川中に位置する合成樹脂は前月比▲0.8％と、８月の同▲2.7％に続いて下落した。内訳をみて
も、ポリエチレンが同▲2.9％、ポリプロピレンが同▲0.2％となるなど、主要品目で下落が続い
ている。４月の川上価格急騰を受けて５～６月には合成樹脂への価格波及が進んだが、夏場以降
は反落が続いている。川中段階での追加的なコスト上昇圧力は弱まりつつあると言えそうだ。 
これに対して、川下価格の動きは対照的である。プラスチック製品は９月も前月比＋1.1％と上

### Page 3

3/4 
昇し、前年比では＋12.8％と８月の＋11.3％からさらに伸びを高めた。川中の合成樹脂価格が２
ヵ月連続で下落する一方、その先に位置するプラスチック製品では前年比上昇率がむしろ加速し
ており、川中と川下の価格動向の乖離が一段と鮮明になった。 
品目別にみても、価格上昇は最終製品に近い段階で続いている。９月はプラスチック製容器
（中空成形を除く）が前月比＋11.2％と大幅に上昇するなど、容器類を中心に価格上昇が続い
た。こうした包装資材は食品や日用品など幅広い商品に用いられるため、川下企業のコスト負担
を高める要因となる。 
こうした川中と川下の価格動向の違いの背景には、企業間の価格転嫁に時間差があることが挙
げられる。原材料価格が低下しても、川下企業の仕入れコストが直ちに下がるわけではない。高
値で調達した原材料の在庫が残っているほか、取引価格の改定も一定の間隔で行われるためであ
る。加えて、物流費や人件費など原材料以外のコスト上昇も続いている。９月の「合成樹脂の下
落とプラスチック製品の上昇」という組み合わせは、既往のコスト高の価格転嫁が川下でなお続
いていることを示すものといえる。 
また、今回の企業物価では、ＡＩ関連需要の拡大を背景とする価格上昇も注目される。９月の
輸出物価指数は契約通貨ベースで前月比＋1.4％となったが、このうち電気・電子機器の寄与度は
＋0.80％ポイントと、上昇の半分以上を占めた。輸入物価でもモス型メモリ集積回路や銅鉱など
が上昇しており、世界的なＡＩ・データセンター需要の拡大による影響がうかがわれる。ＡＩ関
連需要の拡大は日本の輸出や企業収益にとって追い風となる一方、半導体や銅などの調達コスト
上昇を通じて、国内の関連製品価格を押し上げる側面もある。資源価格とは異なる経路からの物
価上昇圧力として、今後の動向が注目される。

3. 川下転嫁と資源高で、物価上昇圧力は長期化へ 
先行きについては、既往のコスト高の川下への価格転嫁が今後もしばらく続く可能性が高い。
川中の合成樹脂価格が下落する一方、川下のプラスチック製品では値上がりが続いており、原材
料価格の低下が川下価格の押し下げにつながるまでには、なお時間を要するとみられる。物流費
や人件費などを含めた企業のコスト水準も依然として高く、価格転嫁の動きは簡単には収まらな
いだろう。 
加えて、資源価格の再上昇による新たなコスト上昇圧力にも注意が必要だ。９月の輸入物価指
数は円ベースでは前月比▲0.2％と小幅な下落となったものの、契約通貨ベースでは同＋1.0％と
上昇に転じた。なかでも石油・石炭・天然ガスは契約通貨ベースで同＋2.3％、円ベースでも同＋
0.9％と上昇している。資源価格再上昇の影響がラグをもって輸入物価押し上げにつながること
で、10 月の輸入物価は上昇に転じる可能性が高まっている。こうした動きが国内の石油・化学関
連価格に時間差を伴って波及すれば、川上からの物価上昇圧力が再び強まることになる。 
このため、国内企業物価の前年比上昇率は９月に＋６％台へ鈍化したものの、今後も当面＋
６％台を中心とした高い伸びが続く可能性が高い。農林水産物の価格下落は引き続き企業物価の
押し下げ要因となる可能性がある一方、既往のコスト高の川下転嫁が続くなか、資源価格や為替

### Page 4

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
4/4
を通じた新たなコスト上昇が重なれば、企業物価の明確な鈍化には時間を要するだろう。
消費者物価への影響にも注意が必要である。米価格の下落は食料品価格を押し下げる要因とな
るものの、米以外の食料品では上昇圧力が強まる可能性が高い。プラスチック製容器など包装資
材の価格上昇が続いているほか、物流費や人件費なども高止まりしており、こうしたコスト増が
時間差を伴って最終販売価格へ転嫁されるとみられる。米価下落が一定の下押し要因となるなか
でも、食料品全体では年度後半にかけて価格上昇圧力が強まると予想している。
総じてみれば、９月の企業物価は、農林水産物や石油製品の下落によって全体の上昇率が鈍化
したものの、川下への価格転嫁はなお続き、ＡＩ関連の価格上昇も目立つ結果となった。先行き

は、既往のコスト高の価格転嫁が続くなか、資源価格の高止まりによって川上からの上昇圧力が
再び強まるリスクもある。企業物価は当面高い伸びが続く可能性が高いだろう。
消費者物価についても、既往のコスト高の価格転嫁が続くことで、上昇圧力が強まるとみられ
る。筆者は、26 年末から27 年初にかけて消費者物価上昇率が前年比＋３％を上回ると予想してお
り、今回の企業物価指数もこうした見通しと整合的な結果だったと考えている。当面、物価の上
振れに警戒が必要な局面が続くだろう。

營 運 分 析 
                   大立光為全球光學鏡頭領導龍頭 
大立光電成立於 1987 年，為全球光學鏡頭與光電零組件領導廠商，核心技
術涵蓋高階模具設計、非球面塑膠鏡片射出成型、多鏡片精密堆疊及全自動
化對位組裝等製程研發。因應智慧型手機市場成長放緩與規格升級趨緩，公
司近年積極推動產品結構優化，將發展重心由傳統手機鏡頭逐步拓展至高附
加價值的共封裝光學、車載鏡頭與醫療光電等領域。透過其微米級精密製造、
獨家主動對準製程與設備全自製整合能力，大立光成功切入光纖陣列單 元
(FAU)、玻塑混合車載鏡頭等高技術門檻供應鏈，致力於開闢第二成長曲線
並建立長期穩定的高毛利業務基礎 ， 逐步降低對單一智慧型手機產業的依賴。

公司 26Q3手機鏡頭出貨量比重：2,000 萬畫素以上佔 10%–20%、1,000 萬
畫素佔 60%–70%、800 萬畫素佔約 10%、500 萬畫素以下佔 20%–30%。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
鏡頭良率逐步改善，但拉貨高峰已過。主因新機開賣後終端銷量未達預期，
客戶自 9 月即開始下修訂單預測；即便客戶預計 27H1 發表之新機種於 10
月提前啟動拉貨，亦無法彌補主力機種之修正缺口。由於客戶目前未提供長
天期訂單預測，27Q1出貨能見度仍相對有限。

中長期規格升級與競爭策略方面，智慧型手機鏡頭的大幅度升級週期預計落
在 2028 年，屆時包含前鏡頭與高階潛望長焦等規格將迎來升級，目前已進
入試產階段；2027 年則以大感光元件的主鏡頭升級為主，具備特定技術門
檻，目前處於送樣階段，產品單價仍待協商。

圖二、全球手機出貨量以及成長率 
全球手機出貨量以及成長率

資料來源：兆豐投顧預測

綠電採購形成結構性成本壓力 
綠電採購方面，大立光依客戶合約規範與其出貨比重採購綠電，已形成長期
結構性成本，為 26Q3 毛利率承壓主因。由於 2027 年全年度產品價格已全
數底定，綠電成本短期內無法即時轉嫁，最快需待 2028 年新機種報價方能
反映，短中期仍對本業毛利形成壓抑。

光通訊佈局全面提速，從 FA升級直供 FAU打造第二引擎 
在產品佈局與量產時程方面，大立光光纖陣列（FA）已獲客戶認證通過。為
降低零組件組裝之責任爭議，公司順應客戶需求，由單純供應 FA 升級為整
合自製 FA、外購微透鏡陣列（PMLA）與稜鏡之完整 FAU 模組，並積極爭
取向下整合封裝至具備高精度定位珠載板。首波產品鎖定單排可插拔式與光
光通訊佈局全面提
速，從 FA 升級直供
FAU打造第二引擎

- 4 - 
個股報告 
柵耦合架構，試產線已完成驗證；首條自研量產線預計於 27Q2 季底完成建
置，並於 27H2 正式量產放量。至於技術難度更高的多排產品目前處於送樣
階段，規劃為 2028 年量產專案。

在產能佈局與核心優勢方面，面對光通訊市場供不應求，公司已調配既有第
二大廠支應 2027 年量產，新購土地則規劃全數拆除重建為高規格無塵室，
預計 2030 年完工以支應長期擴產。核心壁壘上，大立光 FAU精密對位設備
達 100%全段自研自製，憑藉獨家主動對位技術消除外購 V-groove 基板公
差，兼具自動化高速除錯與機台調校彈性。客戶結構方面，當前世代以代工
模式供應單一客戶，2028 年下一世代專案則已直接對接超過一家雲端服務供
應商洽談，確立光通訊為長期第二成長動能。

光通訊成第二成長引擎，業務結構質變推升評價 
預估 26Q4營收為 175.13 億元，QoQ+11.8%、YoY+1.7%；毛利率 42.5%，
QoQ+0.3ppts、YoY-7.5ppts；稅後淨利 51.57億元，QoQ-0.5%、YoY-23.3%；
稅後 EPS 39.43 元。

預估 2026 年營收 623.9 億元，YoY+2%；毛利率 45.7%，YoY-4.7ppts；稅
後淨利 217.23 億元，YoY+3.2%；稅後 EPS 166.08 元。

預估 2027年營收 825.12 億元，YoY+32.3%；毛利率 48.3%，YoY+2.6ppts；
稅後淨利 297.13 億元，YoY+36.8%；稅後 EPS 227.16 元。

公司目前股價位於 29.31X27(F)PER，考量光通業務成長明確，占比提高，
所以取 40X，給予投資評等「買進」，6個月目標價 9100 (40X2027(F)PER)，
潛在上漲空間 36.63%。

宏观周报（ 10.19-10.23） 
金融街论坛释放改革信号 ，日本通胀升温与内需疲弱交织

摘要

西 南证券研究院  
[Table_Summary]  一周大事记  
国内：政策利率拟换锚至隔夜，生猪产能持续去化。10月 19 日，统计局公布
数据显示， 前三季度全国生猪出栏同比增长1.4%， 能繁母猪存栏降至3602万
头，同比下降10.7%。能繁母猪连续两个季度环比下降，生猪产能去化预计在
明年上半年后体现到出栏端与猪价上； 同日， 金融街论坛提出研究将公开市场
主要操作品种由 7 天期调整为隔夜， 推动贷款定价基准多元化及存贷款基准利
率退出。 证监会宣布首批18 只主动 ETF 获批注册， 利率市场化改革与资本市
场产品创新同步推进；20 日至 21 日，APEC 财长会聚焦宏观政策协调、数字
基础设施融资与普惠金融，提出协同缓解融资约束，增强亚太经济韧性；21
日，中办、国办印发《关于推动科技创新和产业创新深度融合的意见》，围绕
职务科技成果转化、科技金融和关键核心技术攻关完善实施机制， 推动创新资
源向企业和产业需求集聚，促进科技成果转化与产业升级衔接。 
海外：日本通胀升温制约政策取舍，银价自年内高点承压回落。当地时间 10
月 20 日，伦敦现货白银收于 57.10美元/盎司，较 1 月年内高点回落 51.79%。
美国通胀反弹对银价形成压力，光伏节银也对工业需求带来影响；21 日，数
据显示日本东京核心CPI 同比上涨 2.7%， 但家庭实际消费支出下降3.1%，通
胀升温与内需疲弱并存， 市场对日本央行在 12 月加息的预期有所增强； 同日，
数据显示韩国 10 月前 20 天出口同比增长 105.4%，半导体出口同比增长
222.5%，但日均出口增速回落至 65.9%。韩国依靠半导体支撑的出口增速的
持续性值得关注； 本周地缘方面，美伊双方军事部署与海运成本有所抬升。美
国增派中东军事力量，海湾航线运费升至冲突前约5 倍，柴油裂解价差也大幅
走高，实际运输成本与炼厂采购需求共同支撑油价上行。 
 高频数据：上游：布伦特原油、阴极铜价格周环比分别下降5.80%、0.88%，
铁矿石价格周环比上升 0.25%；中游：动力煤、水泥价格周环比分别上升
1.94%、3.03%，螺纹钢价格周环比下降0.05%；下游：房地产销售周环比上
升 9.45%，10 月第 3 周乘用车日均零售销量同比下降 29%；物价：蔬菜、猪
肉价格周环比分别下降3.07%、0.62%。新兴产业中，上游：六氟磷酸锂、碳
酸锂价格周环比分别下降 3.42%、 5.96%，国产多晶硅料价格周环比上升
0.29%；中游：DXI指数周环比上升 1.02%，国际散货船价综合指数周环比上
升 0.74%； 下游： 新兴产业中，9 月新能源汽车销量同比上升13.62%，9 月智
能设备销量同比上升 36.00%，平板电脑销量同比下降7.92%。 
 下周重点关注：美国 9 月芝加哥联储全国活动指数、10 月达拉斯联储商业活
动指数（周一）；中国9 月规模以上工业企业利润同比、美国9 月耐用品订单
环比初值、韩国三季度GDP 同比（周二）；美国 9 月商品贸易帐（周三）；
美国 FOMC 利率决策、9 月 PCE 物价指数同比、 欧洲央行利率决策 （周四） ；
日本 10 月东京 CPI、欧元区三季度GDP 季环比初值（周五）。 
 风险提示： 政策落地节奏不及预期，地缘冲突扰动，海外经济超预期波动。
